Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geochemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 444 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 840 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the geochemistry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 444 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 840. QED.
